Peter of Vaux-de-Cernay: Historia Albigensium (1615 Latin edition)
==================================================================
Peter of Vaux-de-Cernay — Historia Albigensium, the Cistercian eyewitness history of the Albigensian Crusade, in the 1615 Latin edition (Troyes: Camusat); public domain. Complete, substituting the unobtainable Sibly 1998 English translation. Source: Internet Archive (Google Books scan).
